Review the changes and make sure they are as expected. Edit the file again if necessary.</tool_response><|assistant|></think><tool_call>str_replace_editor<arg_key>path</arg_key><arg_value>/workspace/spacetelescope__jwst__0.13/jwst/associations/lib/rules_level2_base.py</arg_value><arg_key>new_str</arg_key><arg_value>    @staticmethod
    def _filter_lesser_associations(asns):
        """Filter out lesser associations when product names conflict
        
        When using --all-candidates, both observation associations (starting with 'r') 
        and background associations (starting with 'c') can be produced for the same 
        product names. This method removes the lesser quality associations, preferring
        background associations over observation associations.
        
        Parameters
        ----------
        asns : [association(, ...)]
            List of associations to filter

        Returns
        -------
        associations : [association(, ...)]
            Filtered list of associations with lesser duplicates removed
        """
        # Group associations by product name
        product_groups = {}
        for asn in asns:
            asn_id = asn.data.get('asn_id', '')
            for product in asn['products']:
                product_name = product['name']
                if product_name not in product_groups:
                    product_groups[product_name] = []
                product_groups[product_name].append((asn, asn_id))
        
        # Identify which associations to keep
        keep_associations = set()
        remove_associations = set()
        
        for product_name, assoc_list in product_groups.items():
            if len(assoc_list) <= 1:
                # Only one association for this product, keep it
                for asn, asn_id in assoc_list:
                    keep_associations.add(id(asn))
                continue
                
            # Multiple associations for the same product name
            # Prefer background associations (starting with 'c') over observation (starting with 'r')
            background_assocs = [item for item in assoc_list if item[1].startswith('c')]
            observation_assocs = [item for item in assoc_list if item[1].startswith('r')]
            
            if background_assocs and observation_assocs:
                # Conflict exists - keep background associations, remove observation associations
                for asn, asn_id in background_assocs:
                    keep_associations.add(id(asn))
                for asn, asn_id in observation_assocs:
                    remove_associations.add(id(asn))
            else:
                # No conflict, keep all
                for asn, asn_id in assoc_list:
                    keep_associations.add(id(asn))
        
        # Filter the associations list
        filtered_asns = [
            asn for asn in asns 
            if id(asn) in keep_associations and id(asn) not in remove_associations
        ]
        
        return filtered_asns</arg_value><arg_key>old_str</arg_key><arg_value>    @staticmethod
    def _filter_lesser_associations(asns):
        """Filter out lesser associations when product names conflict
        
        When using --all-candidates, both observation associations (starting with 'r') 
        and background associations (starting with 'c') can be produced for the same 
        product names. This method removes the lesser quality associations, preferring
        background associations over observation associations.
        
        Parameters
        ----------
        asns : [association(, ...)]
            List of associations to filter

        Returns
        -------
        associations : [association(, ...)]
            Filtered list of associations with lesser duplicates removed
        """
        print(f"DEBUG: _filter_lesser_associations called with {len(asns)} associations")
        
        # Group associations by product name
        product_groups = {}
        for asn in asns:
            asn_id = asn.data.get('asn_id', '')
            for product in asn['products']:
                product_name = product['name']
                if product_name not in product_groups:
                    product_groups[product_name] = []
                product_groups[product_name].append((asn, asn_id))
        
        # Identify which associations to keep
        keep_associations = set()
        remove_associations = set()
        
        for product_name, assoc_list in product_groups.items():
            if len(assoc_list) <= 1:
                # Only one association for this product, keep it
                for asn, asn_id in assoc_list:
                    keep_associations.add(id(asn))
                continue
                
            # Multiple associations for the same product name
            # Prefer background associations (starting with 'c') over observation (starting with 'r')
            background_assocs = [item for item in assoc_list if item[1].startswith('c')]
            observation_assocs = [item for item in assoc_list if item[1].startswith('r')]
            
            if background_assocs and observation_assocs:
                # Conflict exists - keep background associations, remove observation associations
                print(f"DEBUG: Conflict found for product '{product_name}': {len(background_assocs)} background, {len(observation_assocs)} observation")
                for asn, asn_id in background_assocs:
                    keep_associations.add(id(asn))
                for asn, asn_id in observation_assocs:
                    remove_associations.add(id(asn))
                    print(f"DEBUG: Marking {asn_id} for removal")
            else:
                # No conflict, keep all
                for asn, asn_id in assoc_list:
                    keep_associations.add(id(asn))

        filtered_asns = [
            asn for asn in asns 
            if id(asn) in keep_associations and id(asn) not in remove_associations
        ]
        
        print(f"DEBUG: Filtered {len(asns)} -> {len(filtered_asns)} associations")

Here's the result of running `cat -n` on a snippet of /workspace/spacetelescope__jwst__0.13/jwst/associations/lib/rules_level2_base.py:
   610	        ]
   611	        return merged_asns
   612	
   613	    @staticmethod
   614	    def _filter_lesser_associations(asns):
   615	        """Filter out lesser associations when product names conflict
   616	        
   617	        When using --all-candidates, both observation associations (starting with 'r') 
   618	        and background associations (starting with 'c') can be produced for the same 
   619	        product names. This method removes the lesser quality associations, preferring
   620	        background associations over observation associations.
   621	        
   622	        Parameters
   623	        ----------
   624	        asns : [association(, ...)]
   625	            List of associations to filter
   626	
   627	        Returns
   628	        -------
   629	        associations : [association(, ...)]
   630	            Filtered list of associations with lesser duplicates removed
   631	        """
   632	        # Group associations by product name
   633	        product_groups = {}
   634	        for asn in asns:
   635	            asn_id = asn.data.get('asn_id', '')
   636	            for product in asn['products']:
   637	                product_name = product['name']
   638	                if product_name not in product_groups:
   639	                    product_groups[product_name] = []
   640	                product_groups[product_name].append((asn, asn_id))
   641	        
   642	        # Identify which associations to keep
   643	        keep_associations = set()
   644	        remove_associations = set()
   645	        
   646	        for product_name, assoc_list in product_groups.items():
   647	            if len(assoc_list) <= 1:
   648	                # Only one association for this product, keep it
   649	                for asn, asn_id in assoc_list:
   650	                    keep_associations.add(id(asn))
   651	                continue
   652	                
   653	            # Multiple associations for the same product name
   654	            # Prefer background associations (starting with 'c') over observation (starting with 'r')
   655	            background_assocs = [item for item in assoc_list if item[1].startswith('c')]
   656	            observation_assocs = [item for item in assoc_list if item[1].startswith('r')]
   657	            
   658	            if background_assocs and observation_assocs:
   659	                # Conflict exists - keep background associations, remove observation associations
   660	                for asn, asn_id in background_assocs:
   661	                    keep_associations.add(id(asn))
   662	                for asn, asn_id in observation_assocs:
   663	                    remove_associations.add(id(asn))
   664	            else:
   665	                # No conflict, keep all
   666	                for asn, asn_id in assoc_list:
   667	                    keep_associations.add(id(asn))
   668	        
   669	        # Filter the associations list
   670	        filtered_asns = [
   671	            asn for asn in asns 
   672	            if id(asn) in keep_associations and id(asn) not in remove_associations
   673	        ]
   674	        
   675	        return filtered_asns
   676	
   677	
   678	# -----------------
   679	# Basic constraints
   680
